Five JSON copies contain the same 707 records; four retained V6/V7 copies contain the same 65 source-based dossiers. All **707 current runtime records map uniquely to actual rows in the ReviewBible `Bestiary.xlsx` Monsters sheet** using its existing naming rules. The workbook has 708 named rows; one is excluded by the existing hidden-name predicate and remains in the original workbook. That predicate was observed, not newly approved or changed. All 7,070 selected scalar comparisons match: size, type, alignment, CR, XP, zone, AC, HP, speed and Save DC. All 707 primary attack names and combat source/draft labels also match their workbook inputs. This proves a bounded provenance chain, not that the workbook's rules agree with the later authoritative source pack. The prior audit's 184 source/runtime differences still stand. The main and ReviewBible primary workbooks each have 19 sheets. Both were read without recalculation or saving. Their corresponding Monsters rows differ in 99 name cells and one Loot Table cell. All 100 differences are retained verbatim in the JSON evidence; no automatic rename, loot substitution or deletion was made. ## Why “trusted” is insufficient evidence The existing ingester sets `draft` to false whenever the Combat Provenance string contains `trusted`. The existing generation scripts choose generic attacks from type-specific pools, including deterministic selection from a hash of the creature's name, and scale values from CR/size bands. - **686 current rows** retain “usable draft” wording in their workbook Traits column. - **436 current rows** retain a NeedsReview note; **425 of those** currently have `combat.draft: false`. - None of the 707 workbook Traits columns is projected into a runtime `traits` field. NeedsReview notes are likewise absent from the runtime rows. - Every runtime defense collection is empty. The current primary workbook has no resistance/immunity columns to supply those collections; the ingester defaults missing defense input to empty lists. The workbook still preserves these notes and inputs. Their absence from the JSON is a projection limitation, not evidence that the concerns were resolved or the information no longer matters. Existing fixed Guardian records and generic combat drafts retain their distinct provenance; this audit does not assign new balance approval to either. ## A rebuild currently has two different outcomes Main and ReviewBible use different naming maps and different metadata-supplement paths, although their current 707-record outputs are identical. The ReviewBible map resolves 20 names that the main map leaves in their older forms. It demonstrates why rerunning the main ingestion command is unsafe as a routine synchronization step: the older and newer names would coexist as separate records. The exact 20 pairs, source rows and naming maps are saved in the evidence. No build was run and neither set of records was removed. ## A source-based projection already exists The retained V6 dataset has **65 Known Descent dossiers**, all matching names in the current merged source pack, and no exact sealed-dossier name matches. The merged source pack deliberately retains all four files. The 65-record projection is useful prior work but cannot simply replace the 707-record runtime file. It has no structured `combat` objects or Save DC fields, omits structured defenses, retains historical absolute `ingestSource` paths, and stores loot under `drop1`…`drop6` with percentage/currency strings. All original files and existing review statuses remain unchanged. Cosmology remains deferred until after verified GitHub delivery. ## What connects The 66 harvest profiles contain 178 component occurrences. Only 34 have a literal current material-name correspondence; 144 occurrences (57 distinct labels) do not. Of the unmatched entries, 117 include a printed tier suffix; removing that suffix produces no additional material-name matches. This audit compares only whitespace and case for identities. Three unmatched Animated Core (Tier 1) occurrences have legacy component-name evidence, but that legacy label points to eleven different current materials. The JSON preserves every contributing recipe/component. It is not a unique alias. The one literal-match tier difference is Pact Devil (Green Court): infernal marrow is printed tier 2, while the global material is tier 3. All 31 literal matches with printed numeric rates and values differ from global rates and values. These are context differences requiring a rule decision, not automatic errors. No source is overwritten and no extra roll or price precedence is inferred. Only two of 40 printed recipe links resolve under current or retained legacy recipe names: | Creature | Printed recipe | Catalog ID | Current operational review | Shared printed ingredient | |---|---|---|---|---| | Season-Bound Autumn | Refined Storm Roast | recipe-92 | reviewed-authored-playtest | sparkling petal | | Sporefolk Adult | Refined Verdant Tackle | recipe-173 | source-only-unreviewed | living root | The other 38 occurrences (30 distinct labels) remain unresolved in this catalog. This does not prove that earlier notebook work or another source never contained those names. Both resolved recipes retain their actual ingredients, formula-access rules and review status; a printed link does not grant a recipe. ## Procedure interfaces Of 64 printed harvest procedures, 36 times differ from the generic body-size table and 56 DCs differ from the generic highest-printed-tier lookup. These comparisons do not establish which procedure controls. Crystal Shardling and Pact Automaton Sentinel lack printed skill/DC/time; those fields remain null. The historical dossier has no invented harvest or stat block. Printed skill labels are Arcana, Religion, Medicine, Nature and Engineering. The reviewed framework distinguishes identification from gathering-trade extraction. Its finite shared anatomy/yield manifest, selected-category timing, preservation and exceptional-drop rules must be reconciled with the creature-specific fields before an executable adapter can be accepted. Of the 34 literal material matches, 31 explicitly list the donor name in the retained global sourceMonsters list. The other three lists are truncated with “and N more”; absence from that shortened list does not disqualify the donor. ## Decisions to confer on 1. For each unresolved harvest label, decide whether it is an unresolved category, a distinct new material, or an explicitly evidenced alias. Do not collapse Animated Core to one of eleven current ingredient names without provenance. Keep all originals and record any approved identity relation separately. 2. Resolve the Pact Devil (Green Court) infernal marrow tier 2 versus global tier 3. Creature-specific values/rates differ from the global reference in all 31 numeric literal matches; agree the applicable context without overwriting source or adding a second independent drop roll. 3. Determine what each printed body-level Harvest skill, Harvest DC and Base time actually governs. The generic framework separates identification and trade extraction, has finite shared yields, and allows explicit component DCs. Confirm whether these body-level fields override it, and at which step. Preserve the two absent procedures until decided. 4. Resolve 38 printed recipe references not found under any current or retained legacy recipe name in this 687-record catalog. Ask the owner/notebook history before declaring the labels nonexistent or superseded. The two resolved names still require all their ordinary components and access rules. 5. Specify a finite per-creature harvest manifest with exact units, quantities, anatomy, weight, preservation, roll dependence and exceptional-drop status. Do not assume global quantity dice multiply every row or that generic and exceptional yields add. Keep restricted materials/formulas behind their existing access routes. 6. Confirm harvest/crafting interpretation only. No source deletion, recipe promotion, runtime installation, publication reclassification or cosmology decision is authorized by this comparison. Cosmology remains deferred until the final joint flow review, successful GitHub push and remote verification. ## Preservation and evidence All 10 bounded controls pass; 278 protected source, candidate, review and runtime files retain their hashes. No recipes were promoted, no source values were changed and no runtime data was installed. - [BESTIARY_CRAFTING_CROSSLINK_AUDIT.json](BESTIARY_CRAFTING_CROSSLINK_AUDIT.json) contains every printed harvest row and recipe reference, matched full material/recipe records, legacy component evidence, procedure comparisons and source hashes. - [Prepared GPT conference](CODEX_BESTIARY_CRAFTING_CROSSLINK_CONFERENCE_PREPARED.txt) contains the actual bounded evidence and questions. Verify the available Action/Bonus Action/Reaction, its exact trigger, components, targets, range and line of effect. No legal target means no activation or payment. A target in an area still needs the card's eligibility. Total cover blocks direct targeting unless expressly overridden.\\n2. Choose a spell from your actual repertoire. Determine its class-specific rank and RP cost: rank 0\\u20139 costs **0/2/3/5/7/10/13/17/21/26**. Add explicitly printed extra costs; Resonance Shield adds 1 RP even when the caster receives it as a cantrip. Generic Overcast is excluded.\\n3. Read Dissonance **before** casting. Limit = max(4, 2 + PB + max(0, casting modifier)). At limit/limit+3/limit+6, ranked spells cost +1/+2/+3 RP. At limit+6 ordinary rank-1+ casting is blocked. Cantrips have no strain surcharge but still build Refrain. An expressly granted traumatic-casting exception pays the cost and its printed trauma; it grants neither rank nor repertoire access.\\n4. Perform its components and establish concentration if required, ending incompatible prior concentration. An ordinary missed, saved-against or countered legal cast refunds nothing and never restores displaced concentration.\\n5. At this cast's beginning, apply ordinary Dissonance and Refrain once: 0 base for rank 0, 1 for ranks 1\\u20135, 2 for ranks 6\\u20139; consecutive same-named casts add Refrain +1/+2/+3, capped at +3. First cast or a different name adds +0. Apply the class's exact protected reduction, routing or vent and any hook whose printed trigger is beginning/casting. Weapon or idle turns do not reset the previous name/Refrain unless the class expressly says so. This chronological commitment occurs before the Counterspell window; a later Reaction cast updates its own history, and resuming the earlier cast does not update that history again.\\n6. Open legal cast-interruption Reactions, resolving nested counters last-in, first-out. A surviving cast then resolves its effect: spell attacks are d20 + PB + the actual casting ability modifier; save DC is 8 + PB + that modifier. Use the exact card's hit/failure/success results and ordinary damage/conditions. Outcome-dependent class hooks occur only at their printed timing and with the required actual result. FL-12 Extraction begins with a genuine decision and movement toward a real Haven/surface destination. Inventory lots, people, casualties, vehicles and provisions. For each cargo lot record identity, owner/custodian, pounds, Load, Extraction Class, priority 1–5, condition, carrier and relevant tags. Before outcomes, the Quartermaster or Leader resolves priority ties; the lowest-priority appropriate lot is put at risk first. Extraction Class is handling danger and attention, not mass, title or a promotion in rarity: | Class | Source examples | |---:|---| | 0 | Ordinary trade goods | | 1 | Common monster parts or durable salvage | | 2 | Uncommon, fragile or regulated cargo | | 3 | Rare artifacts, volatile substances or living captives | | 4 | Very rare strategic cargo or dangerous curses | | 5 | Epic cargo likely to draw sovereign/faction intervention | | 6 | Legendary cargo requiring a dedicated extraction operation | Keep actual Fragile, Volatile, Cursed, Living, Contraband, Perishable and Attention tags. A voluntary passenger is not automatically a Class-3 captive. People retain agency, care needs, legal status and actual consent/restraint facts. A classification grants no authority to bind or own someone. At extraction's start add Pressure equal to **min(3, floor(highest carried Class ÷ 2))** once. Keep the episode's highest charged surcharge. Acquiring higher-Class cargo later adds only the positive difference; dropping or concealing it refunds nothing. Transferring allied cargo, changing transport or renaming the episode cannot renew or erase that history. Secure, hidden and medically/chemically safe are different facts. [Secure Cargo](../BODY_EQUIPMENT_PLAYTEST.md) spends an Action on one feasible handling/protective operation. It supplies no free whole-convoy restraint, automatic success, capacity or lowered Class. A concealed volatile core still needs its real containment. Admit Class-6 cargo only with its declared dedicated handling and route operation. FL-15 Declare Retreat at the start of an actual round when a plausible escape route exists. Keep the same encounter, numbered rounds, positions, conditions, pursuit actors and spent resources. Once per round, one withdrawing lead spends its Action on a fixed-DC escape task using its real method. Begin at route tier DC. A declared superior pursuit/obstruction adds +3, a severe version +6, or actual favorable preparation subtracts 3. Use the Core four task degrees and natural shifts. An exact source can provide a distinct opposed chase instead; do not make both checks for the same uncertainty. | Lead result | Counter gained | |---|---| | Critical Success | 2 Escape | | Success | 1 Escape | | Failure | 1 Pursuit | | Critical Failure | 2 Pursuit | An independently achieved declared round objective, actual sacrificed cargo or blocking maneuver can supply 1 Escape instead of the ordinary lead success. It does not add another generic point. Generic Escape is capped at 1 per round, or 2 when the actual lead critical supplies 2. Losing a real member, route or vehicle can add 1 Pursuit when declared as a consequence; generic Pursuit is capped at 2 per round. Express paid features retain only their exact point permissions. Each round includes one predeclared meaningful opposing threat: a legal attack, actual hazard or necessary sacrifice. It does not add a full enemy multiattack/nova to another chase punishment. Allies use their real Actions to Help, carry, treat, cast, command, move or create obstacles; they do not make extra lead checks. Combat Help keeps its actual advantage and expiry rules. Resolve that round's costs, threat and check once, then compare totals. **Escape at least 3 with Pursuit below 3 succeeds. Pursuit at least 3 fails, including simultaneous 3/3.** Success returns to Expedition Mode with +1 Pressure, actual survivors/cargo and pursuit history. RP, recharge and encounter-use resources do not refresh. Failure gives the opponent its actual advantageous next position or capture opportunity; it does not automatically kill everyone or assign ownership. FL-16 The Expedition roster is not the encounter's participant count. Track who is actually present, participating and able to contribute under their real action/command rules. Reinforcements enter at their actual times; they do not silently grant another unopposed wave. Encounter budgets are provisional planning estimates, not promises of survival or measured balance. Establish objectives, terrain, exits, cargo, civilians, hazards, starting distance and surprise, then use the current combat rules. Cargo protection, escort and retreat require their own stated success conditions rather than only a kill count. Boss Tenacity applies only to a declared source-qualified boss with its actual allowance. Size, cost or an encounter budget does not confer boss status. Use [current control/condition rules](../CONDITIONS_PLAYTEST.md), including exact source counters, replacement and duration. Fear, Reaction denial and ordinary Speed penalties are not interchangeable with full-turn denial."

Its behavior remains rooted in food, territory, nesting, and escape, which gives a prepared Expedition more options than a direct fight. ## What Experienced Expeditions Notice Scratches at the creature’s true height; prey dragged away rather than consumed in place; mineral dust in droppings; repeated avoidance of a seemingly safe route. Prey trails stop abruptly where the animal begins carrying rather than feeding. ## Creature Knowledge Use **Nature** unless the Director approves a more specific approach. | Result | Information Learned | |---|---| | DC 11 | Creature family, usual habitat, and dominant attack type. | | DC 15 | The first reliable counter and the sign that its strongest ability is ready. | | DC 19 | Its defining tactical weakness, safe harvest method, and likely retreat condition. | ## Knowledge Status and Reliability **Status:** Established Field Record **Primary source:** Haven Rangers’ Field Ledger and caravan-master kill reports **Reliability:** High **Last confirmed:** Current Opening and prior guild registries **Confirmed range:** Floors 1-10 **Rumored range:** Regional variants may extend beyond the confirmed range ## Stat Block **Large Beast, Unaligned** **Challenge** 2 (450 XP budget) · **Dungeon Range** T1 · **Floors** 1-10 **Armor** 13 · **Hit Points** 68 · **Speed** 40 ft. | STR | DEX | CON | INT | WIS | CHA | |---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:| | 16 (+3) | 14 (+2) | 13 (+1) | 3 (-4) | 12 (+1) | 6 (-2) | **Resistances** None **Immunities** None **Condition Immunities** None **Tactical Vulnerability** None identified **Save DC** 13 ### Traits **Territorial Morale.** It may retreat or break off if food, young, or escape access is secured. ### Actions **Multiattack.** The creature makes 2 attacks using the actions below. **Mauling Claws.** *Attack:* +5 to hit, one target. *Hit:* 1d10+2 slashing damage (average 8). ## How It Fights It opens with **Mauling Claws** against the target most capable of controlling its movement. It uses **Bite** to punish anyone who tries to stabilize that target. It attacks from concealment, protects its escape path, and stops pursuing once territory or food is secured. ## How to Defeat It **Prepare Before Contact** - Prepare resistance or mitigation for slashing, piercing damage. - Carry bait, scent control, and a noncombat retreat route. **Combat Priority** Control its escape and feeding choices rather than cornering it. **Exploit** The defining weakness is **None identified**. Treat this as an encounter solution rather than an automatic damage vulnerability. Successful Research, Recon, or Resource actions should make the exploit easier or safer. **Withdraw When** Withdraw or change the objective when the creature reaches favorable terrain or isolates a target. A failed retreat against this creature usually costs more than abandoning the immediate treasure. ## Habitat, Goals, and Lair Predators choose terrain that hides approach and protects retreat. The lair is usually more dangerous than the animal. Not every dangerous creature serves a faction or cosmic principle. Ordinary predators become expedition-level threats when dungeon terrain, repeated Openings, or magical food chains reshape them. Animal Handling, food, territory, and nonthreatening movement matter more than language. ## Treasure and Harvest **Harvest skill:** Nature · **Harvest DC:** 10 · **Base time:** 30 minutes | Component | Tier | Drop / Value | |---|---:|---| | Bear meat | 1 | 54%, 12 gp base | | Bear bone shard | 2 | 45%, 24 gp base | **Known recipe links:** Greater Bear Bone Shard Stew. Hide, meat, bone, venom, teeth, and organs are practical resources. Damage type and preservation method determine quality. ## Variants and Encounter Use Change hunger, territory, age, injury, and nesting condition. Environmental behavior is the primary variant system. --- *Compiled from the cited expedition records. Uncertain claims are preserved rather than silently reconciled.* # Pact Automaton Sentinel **Family:** Order Drone **Knowledge status:** Established Field Record **Publication classification:** Public **Dungeon range:** T1, Floors 1-3 **Primary source:** Sentinel maintenance records and battlefield salvage studies **Reliability:** High **Encounter role:** Controller ## What It Is Pact Automaton Sentinel is a distributed containment intelligence. Its bodies are dangerous individually, but the real weapon is the geometry they maintain between one another and the terrain. ## What Experienced Expeditions Notice Evenly spaced scoring on stone; dust cleared in repeated arcs; metallic clicks answering one another around corners; corpses moved out of pathways rather than looted. Loose metal turns to face the same direction. ## Creature Knowledge Use **Engineering or Arcana** unless the Director approves a more specific approach. | Result | Information Learned | |---|---| | DC 11 | Creature family, usual habitat, and dominant attack type. | | DC 15 | The first reliable counter and the sign that its strongest ability is ready. | | DC 19 | Its defining tactical weakness, safe harvest method, and likely retreat condition. | ## Knowledge Status and Reliability **Status:** Established Field Record **Primary source:** Sentinel maintenance records and battlefield salvage studies **Reliability:** High **Last confirmed:** Current Opening and prior guild registries **Confirmed range:** Floors 1-3 **Rumored range:** Regional variants may extend beyond the confirmed range ## Stat Block **Large Construct, Lawful Neutral** **Challenge** 6 (2300 XP budget) · **Dungeon Range** T1 · **Floors** 1-3 **Armor** 18 · **Hit Points** 145 · **Speed** 30 ft. | STR | DEX | CON | INT | WIS | CHA | |---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:| | 17 (+3) | 14 (+2) | 18 (+4) | 8 (-1) | 10 (+0) | 6 (-2) | **Resistances** poison, psychic **Immunities** disease **Condition Immunities** charmed, exhaustion, poisoned **Tactical Vulnerability** forced movement that breaks formation **Save DC** 15 ### Traits **Formation Intelligence.** While within 20 feet of another body in its unit, it gains +1 Armor and cannot be surprised. **Escalation Pulse.** **Containment Pulse** recharges on 5-6; visible signs appear one round before the first use. ### Actions **Multiattack.** The creature makes 2 attacks using the actions below. **Sentinel Halberd.** *Attack:* +7 to hit, one target. *Hit:* 2d10+5 slashing damage (average 16). **Pressure Wave.** One target must succeed on a DC 15 save or take 3d8 force damage and suffer the action’s described control effect. **Containment Pulse (Recharge 5-6).** Creatures in the affected area make a DC 15 Strength save, taking 5d6 force damage (average 18) on a failure and half on a success. The Director should describe the charge-up before resolving it. ## How It Fights It opens with **Sentinel Halberd** against the target most capable of controlling its movement. It uses **Pressure Wave** to punish anyone who tries to stabilize that target. When **Containment Pulse** recharges, it delays only if it can catch a more important cluster or interrupt a decisive ritual. It uses every action to preserve formation and converts broken lines into immediate repositioning. ## How to Defeat It **Prepare Before Contact** - Prepare resistance or mitigation for slashing, force damage. - Spread before **Containment Pulse** becomes available. - Bring forced movement and terrain creation. **Combat Priority** Split the formation and destroy the coordinating body. **Exploit** The defining weakness is **forced movement that breaks formation**. Treat this as an encounter solution rather than an automatic damage vulnerability. Successful Research, Recon, or Resource actions should make the exploit easier or safer. **Withdraw When** Withdraw or change the objective when Containment Pulse catches three or more Expedition members. A failed retreat against this creature usually costs more than abandoning the immediate treasure. ## Habitat, Goals, and Lair Drone zones favor grids, choke points, rotating doors, magnetic floors, and lines that improve formation attacks. Their positions are part of the stat block. Order-Drones are collective constructs designed to solve movement, trespass, and containment problems through formation. A “pair,” “tetrad,” or “quintet” is one tactical intelligence distributed across several bodies. Correct credentials, maintenance codes, or a higher-priority emergency may redirect them. Emotional appeals do nothing unless the automaton was built to interpret them. ## Treasure and Harvest | Component | Tier | Drop / Value | |---|---:|---| | servo node | 1 | Director-set | | memory plate | 1 | Director-set | | conductive wire | 1 | Director-set | Servo nodes, memory plates, conductive wire, command lenses, and intact housings are prime Tinkering and Enchanting components. ## Variants and Encounter Use Change formation size, command priority, and terrain. Each added body should expand tactical geometry rather than simply add attacks. --- *Compiled from the cited expedition records. Uncertain claims are preserved rather than silently reconciled.* Before pricing, identify the actual offered object and quantity, condition, custody, lawful disposition and buyer. Choose one domain: ordinary equipment, reviewed crafted item, ordinary material/trade lot, true artifact, or an unsupported/unvalued object. A grand name, rarity label, high price, ingredient or magical condition does not choose the domain. **Ordinary equipment.** Use its actual grade/size/fitting baseline and the reviewed buyer rules. For an undamaged mundane item to which that schedule applies, the buyer's actual Honored/Liked/Neutral/Disliked/Hated relationship gives 75/60/50/40/25% of baseline; refusal remains possible. Damaged goods need a real condition-specific quote. Gems, jewelry, art and trade goods keep their particular transaction rules. Do not apply an additional Loop multiplier to an already calculated equipment price. These percentages are not magical-item buyback. **Reviewed crafted items.** Use their exact recipe reference and sale restrictions. For the reviewed durable supplemental-body families, use the actual source-batch/Standard-Q plus approved body reference once. Final quality yield, a legacy price, all reusable capital or artifact quality do not replace that reference. Charges, flaw, damage and permitted disposition affect the actual item's quote, without an automatic scrap floor. Retained catalysts and unused output reserves remain separate real stock. **Ordinary nonartifact, nonequipment material/trade lots.** Where no overriding exact quote applies, use: `Base × Provenance × Demand × Condition × Urgency` Base is that lot's actual source reference. If genuinely absent, the Director can establish one finite homogeneous lot using these explicit new provisional references before the offer: | Source band | Preserved lot range | New median reference | |---|---:|---:| | I | 5–15 gp | 10 gp | | II | 15–50 gp | 30 gp | | III | 51–148 gp | 100 gp | | IV | 151–499 gp | 320 gp | | V | 512–1,465 gp | 950 gp | There is no general Tier-VI-or-higher median. These are finite lot values, not per-pound prices or values renewed by splitting a lot. | Factor | Applicable values | |---|---| | Provenance | Undocumented 0.5; disputed 0.75; documented 1; actual Gate-Sealed 1.25 | | Demand | Glut 0.75; normal 1; sought 1.25; strategic 1.5 | | Condition | Damaged 0.5; worn 0.75; sound 1; actually exceptional 1.25 | | Urgency | Forced 0.75; normal 1; real patient auction 1.10 | Apply each factual factor once. An already applied Degraded half-value rule is not halved again as “damaged”; make the duplicate factor 1. Preparation with no price effect does not make material exceptional. A Gate Seal needs actual valid evidence. A patient auction needs its real appointment/buyer and waiting time. A disputed-provenance discount grants no right to sell another owner's property. **True artifacts.** Use the actual artifact formula and profile, with separately stated Base Function Value and Gate Tier Multiplier or a genuine finite buyer quote. Missing inputs mean *unvalued*, not zero or infinite price. No ordinary sword or crafted Masterwork acquires artifact multipliers by being recovered from the dungeon. Record gross and each deduction separately. A negative net estimate exposes a possible liability; it is not a compulsory purchase, debt or negative payment to a seller. Keep exact fractions of copper until each actual final payment. Use 1 sp = 10 cp, 1 gp = 100 cp and 1 pp = 1,000 cp. Where accepted, retain 1 Mithril Mark = 500 cp, Embermark = 2 sp, Soulshard = 1 gp and Tidesilver = 1 sp. Verdant Leaf and otherwise unvalued currency have no invented universal conversion. Names do not establish currency origins. Round a final purchase, tax or fee payable **up** to copper once; round a final sale receipt **down** once. A coherent homogeneous sale bill is one receipt. Distinct real contracts can have separate final rounding; individual factors or components are not new transactions merely to change rounding. Displayed decimals never replace the exact value. For each entry, record transaction ID, payer/payee, currency, goods/quantity or service, agreement/authority, exact quote, actual rounded payment, timestamp, status and related prior payment. Quoted, reserved, paid, owed, refunded and lawfully written off are different states. A price quote spends nothing. Apply deposits to their agreed bill. Insurance is an inflow, not sold cargo. For the default Loop net-share sponsor agreement: `SponsorBase = max(0, G − T − RQ)` `SponsorShare = contracted percentage × SponsorBase, rounded once as payment` `NetExpedition = G − T − RQ − SponsorShare` G is actual sale receipts, T the direct Haven taxes on them, and RQ the episode's actual mandatory rescue/quarantine expenses. Count RQ once whether paid earlier or now. This sponsor base is before personal wages and discretionary purchases. A signed gross-share or priority-lien agreement instead uses its own declared terms. True profit includes actual episode inflows less every distinct actual episode cost once: shares, wages, consumed/replaced supplies, operating fuel, repair, interest and other agreed costs. Show capital purchases and borrowed principal separately. Loans and principal repayment are financing, and selling existing personal capital is not earned cargo. Cash allocated to next-expedition reserves is a further allocation, not automatically the same as profit. Report the charter's gross cargo target, net expedition target and true-profit/distributable-after-reserves target, stating whether the comparison is an appraisal or a realized sale. Appraised value does not pay wages. Keep insufficiently funded obligations owed; do not invent negative inventory, a compulsory new loan or unlimited sponsor funds. Allocations cannot exceed actual available money."

[angry-grief-bound,hungry-grief-bound,lonely-grief-bound] Grief-Bound are emotional deaths that found enough matter or Resonance to keep moving. They are not ordinary ghosts. Each is a self-reinforcing need that hunts situations capable of repeating the moment that made it. [angry-grief-bound,hungry-grief-bound,lonely-grief-bound] Some can be quieted, redirected, or released if the original loss is understood. False promises often produce a stronger return. [angry-grief-bound] **Known recipe links:** Transcendent Stone Garment. [angry-grief-bound,hungry-grief-bound,lonely-grief-bound] Residual emotion can be captured as grief-salt, memory ash, or a bound echo. Such material is useful in Scribing, Enchanting, and necromantic research but carries contamination risk.     - generic: "ows its own finite procedure; this generic harvest does not refresh those uses. Humanoid and celestial remains keep the source's consent, lawful-authority or explicit-dark-path restriction; the procedure supplies no blanket permission and makes no cosmological conclusion. ## Preservation and spoilage **Source:** blood/organs 1–4 hours, essence 30 minutes–2 hours unless contained, meat 8–24 hours, hides 12–24 hours before curing; dry bone/horn/teeth/mineral and ordinary construct parts can last indefinitely. Active cores are exceptions. **Completion:** the manifest fixes the interval before harvesting. Where no more specific interval is stated, use the conservative low end: organs/blood 1 hour, essence 30 minutes, meat 8 hours, hide 12 hours. The clock starts when the creature dies or the material leaves its sustaining environment, not when the player finally harvests it. Track condition separately from material tier and rarity: Fresh → Degraded → Spoiled. At one unprotected interval the lot becomes Degraded: its unit value halves and it cannot meet a recipe calling for Fresh ingredients, but otherwise retains its exact tier/properties. At two unprotected intervals it is Spoiled, value zero and unusable as an ordinary recipe ingredient or edible food. A specific recipe may explicitly require spoiled material. No new poison, curse or disease is inferred. Dividing, trading, repackaging, recovering or reharvesting a lot cannot reset its elapsed age. The label quality here is preservation condition, never an equipment grade or crafting outcome. Work on a perishable ingredient must begin while it is eligible; ordinary reservation does not suspend aging. An exact recipe card may say its first processing phase stabilizes the input; otherwise it must remain eligible through completion. A generic engine cannot promise a 160-hour potion made from an untreated one-hour organ will succeed. This makes the card's storage/processing details mandatory. If a required reserved component ceases to satisfy its recipe requirement before completion and no completed recipe phase expressly stabilized it, pause the order before further active work. Before resuming, the lead may reserve a fresh legal component satisfying that same requirement, including an already-legal substitution. The replacement lot keeps its own identity and age. Completed active-work phases remain completed, but any component already physically consumed or irreversibly processed before becoming unusable remains spent. If no legal replacement is supplied, abandon the order under the normal abandonment rule; do not make a completion or quality roll. The following new mundane preservation procedure supplies a usable default without inventing magical stasis. For each lot of at most 10 lb of eligible blood/organs, meat or hide, spend 30 active minutes, a suitable trade kit, a sealable appropriately sized container, and 1 gp/0.5 lb of preservative supplies (salt, clean packing and the appropriate nonmagical stabilizer). These supplies have no independent combat or magical effect. Alchemy handles specimens, Cooking handles food, and Skinning handles hide. Make one DC 10 check using the trade's normal ability/proficiency, no assistants or quality-yield table. Success pauses spoilage for 24 elapsed hours; failure uses the supplies and time but gives no extension. This treatment must finish before the lot becomes Spoiled. It does not restore Fresh condition or remove existing age. On a successful treatment, freeze accumulated unprotected age and resume it after the 24 hours. A replacement treatment takes the same cost/time and extends protection from its completion to 24 hours later, never adding stacked days. Any actual container breach, contamination or incompatible heat ends protection; merely opening to portion with clean tools for less than a minute does not. Preserved supplies and container mass count as cargo until discarded or used. Processing into food is not automatically safe if the source explicitly says otherwise. Mundane specimen jars alone contain fluids but do not pause decay. They do not stabilize magical essence or active cores. Those require a separately defined compatible containment method with its own lifetime, capacity and costs; without one, the printed/default clock continues. Dry durable materials remain indefinite only while kept dry and physically intact. No generic refrigeration, freezing or stasis benefit is inferred from a spell name; its actual reviewed effect must support the storage method. ## Salvage, research and sale Do not confuse harvesting remains, manufacturing a Salvaging-tool recipe, and dismantling an existing item. A salvage tool's particular bonus requires its reviewed card. Ordinary failure recovery is already part of the batch check and cannot be claimed again as dismantling that failed batch. **New basic dismantling procedure:** spend 1 active hour per item of at most 50 lb with actual Salvager's tools, a stable work surface, and applicable proficiency. Larger or magical/unique items require an explicit salvage profile first. Before work begins, record each recoverable ordinary component and the maximum quantity physically remaining in the item after known prior damage, loss or consumption. On Success, recover 25% of each recorded recoverable quantity, rounded down under that component's normal unit rule. Explicitly divisible bulk material may use the framework's 0.01-source-unit precision. Failure recovers none. No later appraisal may increase the predeclared recoverable cap. An unknown bill of materials gives no invented valuable component. Roll INT or WIS plus the one applicable PB contribution against DC 10. The item is irreversibly dismantled on either result. No general recovery of catalysts, expended ammunition/consumables, enchanting power, RP, souls, formulas or previously missing parts. No sale-value-to-free-material conversion. This check uses binary success; no natural-die degree bonus or extra recovery is introduced. If there are no known recoverable materials, ordinary scrap may be described but has no assigned recipe identity or automatic resale value. **Source:** experiment with a stated goal, compatible components, station and research check; failed research can create notes. **Completion:** researching an existing available formula uses its `minimumResearchHours`, minimum 8 active hours, and a copy/sample or credible source sufficient for that knowledge. It uses the trade ability with proficiency against that recipe's DC, plus explicit applicable research bonuses. Success yields a usable transcription of the exact formula if the source actually contains enough information; failure yields notes, no formula, and consumes those research hours. No automatic ingredient consumption unless a destructive sample test is declared with actual quantities before research. Do not reroll unchanged evidence; a fresh attempt needs a new source/sample or eight additional hours of a concretely different method. Copying an already legible legitimately accessible formula requires 1 hour and 1 sp of writing supplies per recipe and no check, and grants no trade XP. These routes grant knowledge, not item effects or access through a faction's restrictions. Designing a new recipe, a unique substitution, or reverse-engineering an unknown magical item yields a proposal for a new reviewed card. It cannot automatically invent a numerical power from a high roll. State the desired result, consumed test materials, facility, hours, DC and what information success can actually reveal before experimentation. This is an authoring boundary, not cosmology research or permission to expose Director lore. **Batch pricing completion:** `recommendedMarketValue` is the recommendation for the Standard batch. A nominal unit reference value is batch value divided by Standard batch quantity; keep exact precision until the final transaction. Market recommendation is not a guaranteed buyer or profit. Actual quotes specify units, finished quality, condition, availability, legal claims and demand. Ordinary crafted mundane equipment uses the already reviewed mundane resale standing percentages. Actual artifacts use the Core artifact market procedure only when their reviewed profiles classify them as artifacts. The frameworks are not multiplied together indiscriminately. Crafting quality alone supplies no extra sale multiplier. The source commission default is a 50% deposit on the agreed complete quote, plus delivery of all agreed irreplaceable monster parts before work. The quote explicitly states who supplies other ingredients, whether those costs are included, delivery schedule, acceptable quality and failure/replacement terms. There is no universal refund or loss of all funds inferred from a failed roll. Record these terms before accepting a commission in the fiction. Do not charge the same supplied ingredient twice or turn returned unused ingredients into a second payout. Use reviewed equipment rounding: final purchase cost up to a copper; final sale proceeds down to a copper, once. A buyer commits to a finite quoted quantity and price before mass-production plans rely on that sale. Once it is filled, more production needs another actual buyer/quote; a reference price does not generate infinite demand. Existing Core/local market procedures can change later quotes. Generic crafting does not manufacture gold, create a guaranteed arbitrage floor, or silently apply the artifact market to every mundane crafted object. The older expedition 0–3 Integrity scale does not replace reviewed structural HP for personal equipment or this chapter's kits. Use the equipment repair procedure for those actual items. Convoy/vehicle Integrity, wider Haven services, Supply conversion and the full expedition loop remain separate integration work; this supplement does not claim they are completed. ## Worked cases and release boundary - A Standard recipe-1 batch reserves the printed ingredients once, takes 1 active hour and lists 3 outputs with a 54-gp batch reference (18 gp each). Its resistance effect and actual output card remain separately unreviewed. A Mishap returns none of those consumed inputs, while the separate reusable kit remains subject only to its actual mishap. - A Forge Dwarf's eligible 8-hour artisan order takes 6 active hours; a Long Rest still takes 8 hours. Two qualified assistants would each need to spend the same actual 6 hours if that recipe permits two assistants. - A Medium body with four categories and a total yield of 3 units allocates those units before selection. Choosing one category uses 8 minutes (30/4 rounded up) and forfeits the other ordinary categories. It cannot yield all three units from each category. - Blood with a 1-hour interval dies at 09:00 and is harvested at 09:20. Untreated it degrades at 10:00 and spoils at 11:00. Successful treatment ending at 09:50 freezes 50 minutes of age until 09:50 the next day; degradation then occurs after 10 further unprotected minutes.     - generic: "82 dict_keys(['recipeId', 'name', 'visibility', 'status', 'sourceRecipe', 'publicBookBinding', 'operational']) Harvest exact ## Quality and finite output These are **crafting outcome labels**, separate from equipment grade and Core artifact market condition. An Exceptional craft does not automatically become Exceptional equipment (+3 statistics), and a Masterwork craft does not create a new grade or a magic item. An item named Superior does not obtain equipment-grade bonuses unless its reviewed profile explicitly binds that grade. Resolve this specific six-band crafting table instead of general expedition outcome shifts. A natural 1 is a Mishap even if the total is high. A natural 20 uses its numerical total with no extra band shift. A margin of −10 or lower is a Mishap. | Total minus DC | Result | Procedure | |---|---|---| | 10 or more | Masterwork | Choose 150% batch yield, rounded down, or the improvement option below; also preserve one eligible catalyst unit. | | 5 through 9 | Exceptional | Choose 125% batch yield, rounded down, or the improvement option below. | | 0 through 4 | Standard | Listed number of ordinary finished units. | | −1 through −4 | Flawed | If base batch quantity is at least 2, produce half quantity rounded down, with ordinary unit effects. If quantity is 1, make one item with a defined flaw below. | | −5 through −9 | Failed | No product; recover 25% of consumed non-catalyst input quantity for each material identity, rounded down to that material's permitted unit. | | −10 or less, or natural 1 | Mishap | No product; lose consumed inputs, return nonconsumed tools, and roll the mishap procedure. | **Completion:** quality choices are declared after the check, once for the whole batch. Extra yield does not itself consume another batch of ingredients; it represents reduced processing waste. For physical outputs the card must specify lawful packaging and output mass; total output cannot exceed actual retained material plus explicitly supplied extra mundane carrier/packaging. A yield increase cannot create missing bottles, duplicate a unique named core, or duplicate the existing item being enchanted. Such an order uses its legal improvement option or retains Standard quantity and its quality label. Floor arithmetic may give no extra units; no fractional item or banked remainder is created. The default improvement option increases explicitly defined maximum structural item HP by 25% (Exceptional) or 50% (Masterwork), rounded down, minimum +1. Current HP increases by the same amount for the new undamaged item. It never increases creature HP, AC, damage, save DC, spell rank, RP, charges or capacity. A recipe card may replace this with one precise property/charge option reviewed for that recipe. With neither known structural HP nor a reviewed improvement option, retain Standard output and the quality label only. Consumables do not gain reusable doses or longer durations from this generic option. A quantity-1 Flawed durable item has maximum structural HP equal to half its ordinary maximum, rounded down, minimum 1, and begins at that reduced maximum. This reduced maximum is a crafting flaw, not ordinary structural damage. Ordinary equipment repair may restore current structural HP only up to that reduced maximum; it does not remove the flaw, increase the item's maximum, alter its equipment grade or convert it to Standard quality. Removing a crafting flaw requires an explicit reviewed rework procedure; absent one, the reduced maximum persists. A quantity-1 consumable whose useful effect has a positive finite duration instead has half that duration, rounded down to a whole round (six seconds), minimum one round. Instant healing/damage/RP restoration and otherwise incompatible results have no generic flaw profile: treat that Flawed result as Failed with the normal 25% non-catalyst recovery. Do not halve an undefined feature or invent a permanent debuff. The completed output card must state whether either default applies. Masterwork preserves **one component unit total** bearing role catalyst and consumed=true, chosen after the check. It must be an actual catalyst used in this batch, not a named irreplaceable component whose exact formula requires destruction; an indivisible catalyst cannot be split. Return that unit to inventory unchanged rather than deducting it. With no eligible catalyst, nothing is returned. The ingredient's exact identity, tier and remaining shelf life persist; this is not a fresh item. Nonconsumed tools always remain unless a specific mishap physically damages one. **Reviewed Forge Dwarf completion:** when eligible artisan-tool creation achieves Standard, treat its output as Exceptional for the yield/improvement choice. Already Exceptional/Masterwork remains so. Failed, Flawed and Mishap do not upgrade. This gives the ancestry's quality language a bounded crafting function; it grants no equipment grade, price multiplier, success guarantee or extra XP. ## Mishaps The public source references a tradeskill mishap table but contains none. **Completion:** after losing consumed inputs, roll 1d6 on this shared table. The trade reference supplies the injury damage type, giving every trade an executable table. A Mishap still never destroys a formula or an irreplaceable tool unless the damage below actually reduces that tool to 0 HP. Cleanup/calibration time is extra active time after the failed batch, not part of its completion hours. | d6 | Result | |---|---| | 1 | Spilled workspace: 1 active hour of cleanup before this station can be used again. | | 2 | Miscalibration: the primary kit cannot support another crafting check until someone proficient with it spends 2 active hours recalibrating it. No extra ingredients are required. | | 3 | Tool damage: the lead's primary kit takes 1 point of compatible physical structural damage, using its actual object defenses; if that kit cannot physically suffer it, use result 1 instead. | | 4 | Minor accident: the lead takes 1d4 damage of the trade's listed injury type, resolved normally through defenses, with no extra condition. Assistants take none. | | 5 | Fouled apparatus: the station requires cleanup labor equal to 1 active labor-hour × the number of workers who participated in the failed order. Any physically capable workers may perform that cleanup; multiple workers may contribute simultaneously. The station cannot support another crafting order until the total required cleanup labor is completed. | | 6 | Lost notes: this order yields no failure-research XP; the lead must spend 1 active hour reconstructing its work log before beginning another order in this trade. The owned formula remains intact. | No ordinary mishap automatically creates a curse, disease, Strain, cosmological revelation, persistent hazard or lost proficiency. A specific reviewed volatile component can supply a separately declared hazard before the order starts. Do not invent that hazard after the roll. The Director describes the accident according to the actual trade and ingredients. ## Trade XP and specialties **Source:** first successful craft 4 XP; nontrivial repeat 2; difficult failed attempt with usable research 1; trivial mass production 0. Advancing from level L to L+1 costs 10+L trade XP. Maximum level 20. The source per-recipe 4-XP field is not an extra award on top of this system. **Completion:** a successful craft means Standard, Exceptional or Masterwork. Award 4 for the first success of a recipe ID in that trade if not trivial; subsequent nontrivial successes award 2. A recipe is trivial when current trade level is at least `trivialAtSkillLevel` (all source thresholds are recipe skill level +5). Triviality wins even for a previously unmade recipe. Changing ingredients, quality, batch size, location or a source alias does not reset first-success history. A Flawed/Failed/Mishap order may give 1 XP only when its recipe is at least the crafter's current trade level, it consumed actual inputs and hours, and the lead records a concrete usable research finding. This is once per recipe ID per trade level, not repeat failure farming; mishap 6 gives none. No assistant or uninvolved observer receives this work-order award. Untrained attempts record experience narratively but bank no numerical trade XP and grant no proficiency. Training initializes zero XP. Spend XP immediately when a threshold is reached; carry excess toward the next level, repeat if enough, and stop at 20. At level 10 select one concrete specialty such as antidotes, metal shields or textile containers. Record its scope before the next check; it cannot be renamed for each work order. Its +1 applies once. Further specialties, retraining and unique substitutions require another explicit feature or agreed research result; the rank alone grants none. docs/bestiary/Decennium_Descent_Monster_Manual_Canon_Rewrite_Source_Pack/Monster_Manual_of_the_Known_Descent_Source/entries/sporefolk-adult.md ## Identify and harvest **Source:** identify with the creature type's knowledge skill, then extract using a relevant gathering trade. The type mapping and size times/yields are in the trade reference. This procedure concerns actual remains and environmental nodes already present; it does not create a creature, corpse, mineral vein, legal entitlement or formula. **Completion:** the Director records a finite harvest manifest before checks: actual accessible categories, unit identities/tier/weight/value, quantities or yield dice, damaged/unavailable parts, preservation deadlines, and any exceptional drop. Normally expose four to six plausible categories, but a creature with fewer real categories has fewer. These are choices within one finite yield pool, not four to six copies of the yield roll. A Tiny creature cannot produce a Large creature's organ or an unlimited number of crystals. Use the exact component's explicit harvest DC when present; otherwise the highest desired material tier sets DC: I 12, II 13, III 15, IV 16, V 18, VI 19. These are the existing expedition tier DCs, not recipe crafting DCs. The generic table has no public Tier VII–X harvest entitlement. A specially restricted ingredient beyond this table needs a separately stated DC and access procedure before rolling. Identification takes 1 minute of close examination. Use INT and the source type skill except Medicine uses WIS; PB requires actual relevant training, with existing task-scoped expertise replacing it. One lead and at most one trained assistant (+1) can participate. A success reveals the manifest's usable categories, hazards and preservation needs; failure identifies no hidden qualities. There is no invented false fact on failure. The party may still extract a plainly recognizable category such as a hide, using the declared DC with disadvantage while unidentified. Further identification attempts require new evidence, a different method or consultation, not repeated rolls by every party member on unchanged evidence. Identification does not itself extract anything. The extraction lead selects a compatible gathering trade: Skinning for animal parts, Herbalism for plants, Mining for mineral deposits, Fishing for aquatic gathering, Salvaging for constructs and dismantled devices; a specific listed source may authorize another. Use that trade's ability and primary-tool rules. Recipe-level access gates do not apply to an unranked generic harvest; actual restricted material access still does. Up to one eligible assistant gives +1 and spends the same extraction time. Tools and access are required; no station is required for ordinary physical field removal beyond a stable accessible work surface and appropriate containers. A kit cannot contain an unsafe magical essence without a suitable actual vessel. | Size | Full extraction time | Base total units | |---|---|---| | Tiny | 10 minutes | 1 | | Small | 20 minutes | 1d2 | | Medium | 30 minutes | 1d4 | | Large | 1 hour | 2d4 | | Huge | 2 hours | 4d4 | | Gargantuan | 4 hours | 8d4 | Roll base yield once for the remains when the manifest is made. It is the shared total of ordinary units across categories, constrained by actual anatomy and mass. An explicit bestiary drop uses its own finite chance/quantity, once, in addition only when the source calls it an exceptional separate drop. A global material's dropRate does not add another independent roll for every identical name in the material catalog. State which exceptional drops depend on a successful extraction before rolling. Full extraction uses the listed time. For a partial harvest, select which manifest categories to attempt first. Time is full time × chosen categories / available categories, rounded up to whole minutes, minimum 1 minute. Allocation of the finite units across categories is fixed before selection, so selecting one category cannot redirect the entire pool into its most valuable part. Once partial harvesting begins, unselected ordinary categories are forfeited for this generic procedure; another worker cannot harvest the same remainder again. An explicit unique drop can retain its separately stated procedure. Use the core four-degree check for extraction: total ≥DC+10 critical success, ≥DC success, >DC−10 failure, ≤DC−10 critical failure; natural 20 improves one degree and natural 1 worsens one. Success recovers the selected allocation. Critical success additionally preserves one otherwise damaged ordinary unit within the selected attempted allocation that the manifest already recorded as damaged but potentially recoverable. If the selected allocation contains no such unit, Critical Success grants no additional unit. It cannot recover an unselected or already-forfeited category, duplicate an exceptional drop or exceed the manifest's finite anatomy. Failure ruins one selected unit (highest-value selected unit, tied at player's choice), recovers no selected units, and ends that attempt. Critical failure ruins all selected ordinary units, recovers none, and invokes only a hazard explicitly declared in the manifest; absent one, apply the gathering trade's minor accident (1d4 damage) to the lead. No reroll or new generic attempt on the same exhausted selection; named exceptional-drop procedures remain as stated. A hurried extraction takes half the listed/partial time, rounded up to whole minutes, minimum 1 minute, with disadvantage. This is a new reconciliation of the detailed size table with the older one-hour rushed Watch summary; it avoids making a hurried Tiny harvest slower than normal. Normal and hurried harvesting consume actual Watch time, not a second automatic eight-hour charge. Yield, limits and spoilage are unchanged. Resource Team membership grants no duplicate attempt or extra yield. Harvested lots become actual cargo immediately, including containers and weight. Food, water or fuel gathered separately does not magically become the complete expedition Supply bundle. An ancestry's explicit forage or Natural Remedy grant follows its own finite procedure; this generic harvest does not refresh those uses. Humanoid and celestial remains keep the source's consent, lawful-authority or explicit-dark-path restriction; the procedure supplies no blanket permission and makes no cosmological conclusion."

Before outcomes, the Quartermaster or Leader resolves priority ties; the lowest-priority appropriate lot is put at risk first.\\n\\nExtraction Class is handling danger and attention, not mass, title or a promotion in rarity:\\n\\n| Class | Source examples |\\n|---:|---|\\n| 0 | Ordinary trade goods |\\n| 1 | Common monster parts or durable salvage |\\n| 2 | Uncommon, fragile or regulated cargo |\\n| 3 | Rare artifacts, volatile substances or living captives |\\n| 4 | Very rare strategic cargo or dangerous curses |\\n| 5 | Epic cargo likely to draw sovereign/faction intervention |\\n| 6 | Legendary cargo requiring a dedicated extraction operation |\\n\\nKeep actual Fragile, Volatile, Cursed, Living, Contraband, Perishable and Attention tags. A voluntary passenger is not automatically a Class-3 captive. People retain agency, care needs, legal status and actual consent/restraint facts. A classification grants no authority to bind or own someone.\\n\\nAt extraction's start add Pressure equal to **min(3, floor(highest carried Class ÷ 2))** once. Keep the episode's highest charged surcharge. Acquiring higher-Class cargo later adds only the positive difference; dropping or concealing it refunds nothing. Transferring allied cargo, changing transport or renaming the episode cannot renew or erase that history.\\n\\nSecure, hidden and medically/chemically safe are different facts. A concealed volatile core still needs its real containment. Admit Class-6 cargo only with its declared dedicated handling and route operation.\"} FL-15 {\"id\": \"FL-15\", \"title\": \"Retreat without restarting the encounter\", \"text\": \"Declare Retreat at the start of an actual round when a plausible escape route exists. Keep the same encounter, numbered rounds, positions, conditions, pursuit actors and spent resources.\\n\\nOnce per round, one withdrawing lead spends its Action on a fixed-DC escape task using its real method. Begin at route tier DC. A declared superior pursuit/obstruction adds +3, a severe version +6, or actual favorable preparation subtracts 3. Use the Core four task degrees and natural shifts. An exact source can provide a distinct opposed chase instead; do not make both checks for the same uncertainty.\\n\\n| Lead result | Counter gained |\\n|---|---|\\n| Critical Success | 2 Escape |\\n| Success | 1 Escape |\\n| Failure | 1 Pursuit |\\n| Critical Failure | 2 Pursuit |\\n\\nAn independently achieved declared round objective, actual sacrificed cargo or blocking maneuver can supply 1 Escape instead of the ordinary lead success. It does not add another generic point. Generic Escape is capped at 1 per round, or 2 when the actual lead critical supplies 2. Losing a real member, route or vehicle can add 1 Pursuit when declared as a consequence; generic Pursuit is capped at 2 per round. Express paid features retain only their exact point permissions.\\n\\nEach round includes one predeclared meaningful opposing threat: a legal attack, actual hazard or necessary sacrifice. It does not add a full enemy multiattack/nova to another chase punishment. Allies use their real Actions to Help, carry, treat, cast, command, move or create obstacles; they do not make extra lead checks. Combat Help keeps its actual advantage and expiry rules.\\n\\nResolve that round's costs, threat and check once, then compare totals. **Escape at least 3 with Pursuit below 3 succeeds. Pursuit at least 3 fails, including simultaneous 3/3.** Success returns to Expedition Mode with +1 Pressure, actual survivors/cargo and pursuit history. RP, recharge and encounter-use resources do not refresh. Failure gives the opponent its actual advantageous next position or capture opportunity; it does not automatically kill everyone or assign ownership.\"} FL-16 {\"id\": \"FL-16\", \"title\": \"Estimate encounters from actual participants\", \"text\": \"The Expedition roster is not the encounter's participant count. Track who is actually present, participating and able to contribute under their real action/command rules. Reinforcements enter at their actual times; they do not silently grant another unopposed wave.\\n\\nEncounter budgets are provisional planning estimates, not promises of survival or measured balance. Establish objectives, terrain, exits, cargo, civilians, hazards, starting distance and surprise, then use the current combat rules. Cargo protection, escort and retreat require their own stated success conditions rather than only a kill count.\\n\\nBoss Tenacity applies only to a declared source-qualified boss with its actual allowance. Size, cost or an encounter budget does not confer boss status. Use [current control/condition rules](../CONDITIONS_PLAYTEST.md), including exact source counters, replacement and duration. Fear, Reaction denial and ordinary Speed penalties are not interchangeable with full-turn denial.\"} FL-20 {\"id\": \"FL-20\", \"title\": \"Choose one valuation method\", \"text\": \"Before pricing, identify the actual offered object and quantity, condition, custody, lawful disposition and buyer. Choose one domain: ordinary equipment, reviewed crafted item, ordinary material/trade lot, true artifact, or an unsupported/unvalued object. A grand name, rarity label, high price, ingredient or magical condition does not choose the domain.\\n\\n**Ordinary equipment.** Use its actual grade/size/fitting baseline and the reviewed buyer rules. For an undamaged mundane item to which that schedule applies, the buyer's actual Honored/Liked/Neutral/Disliked/Hated relationship gives 75/60/50/40/25% of baseline; refusal remains possible. Damaged goods need a real condition-specific quote. Gems, jewelry, art and trade goods keep their particular transaction rules. Do not apply an additional Loop multiplier to an already calculated equipment price. These percentages are not magical-item buyback.\\n\\n**Reviewed crafted items.** Use their exact recipe reference and sale restrictions. For the reviewed durable supplemental-body families, use the actual source-batch/Standard-Q plus approved body reference once. Final quality yield, a legacy price, all reusable capital or artifact quality do not replace that reference. Charges, flaw, damage and permitted disposition affect the actual item's quote, without an automatic scrap floor. Retained catalysts and unused output reserves remain separate real stock.\\n\\n**Ordinary nonartifact, nonequipment material/trade lots.** Where no overriding exact quote applies, use:\\n\\n`Base × Provenance × Demand × Condition × Urgency`\\n\\nBase is that lot's actual source reference. If genuinely absent, the Director can establish one finite homogeneous lot using these explicit new provisional references before the offer:\\n\\n| Source band | Preserved lot range | New median reference |\\n|---|---:|---:|\\n| I | 5–15 gp | 10 gp |\\n| II | 15–50 gp | 30 gp |\\n| III | 51–148 gp | 100 gp |\\n| IV | 151–499 gp | 320 gp |\\n| V | 512–1,465 gp | 950 gp |\\n\\nThere is no general Tier-VI-or-higher median. These are finite lot values, not per-pound prices or values renewed by splitting a lot.\\n\\n| Factor | Applicable values |\\n|---|---|\\n| Provenance | Undocumented 0.5; disputed 0.75; documented 1; actual Gate-Sealed 1.25 |\\n| Demand | Glut 0.75; normal 1; sought 1.25; strategic 1.5 |\\n| Condition | Damaged 0.5; worn 0.75; sound 1; actually exceptional 1.25 |\\n| Urgency | Forced 0.75; normal 1; real patient auction 1.10 |\\n\\nApply each factual factor once. An already applied Degraded half-value rule is not halved again as “damaged”; make the duplicate factor 1. Preparation with no price effect does not make material exceptional. A Gate Seal needs actual valid evidence. A patient auction needs its real appointment/buyer and waiting time. A disputed-provenance discount grants no right to sell another owner's property.\\n\\n**True artifacts.** Use the actual artifact formula and profile, with separately stated Base Function Value and Gate Tier Multiplier or a genuine finite buyer quote. Missing inputs mean *unvalued*, not zero or infinite price. No ordinary sword or crafted Masterwork acquires artifact multipliers by being recovered from the dungeon. Record gross and each deduction separately. A negative net estimate exposes a possible liability; it is not a compulsory purchase, debt or negative payment to a seller.\"} FL-21 {\"id\": \"FL-21\", \"title\": \"One settlement ledger\", \"text\": \"Keep exact fractions of copper until each actual final payment. Use 1 sp = 10 cp, 1 gp = 100 cp and 1 pp = 1,000 cp. Where accepted, retain 1 Mithril Mark = 500 cp, Embermark = 2 sp, Soulshard = 1 gp and Tidesilver = 1 sp. Verdant Leaf and otherwise unvalued currency have no invented universal conversion. Names do not establish currency origins.\\n\\nRound a final purchase, tax or fee payable **up** to copper once; round a final sale receipt **down** once. A coherent homogeneous sale bill is one receipt. Distinct real contracts can have separate final rounding; individual factors or components are not new transactions merely to change rounding. Displayed decimals never replace the exact value.\\n\\nFor each entry, record transaction ID, payer/payee, currency, goods/quantity or service, agreement/authority, exact quote, actual rounded payment, timestamp, status and related prior payment. Quoted, reserved, paid, owed, refunded and lawfully written off are different states. A price quote spends nothing. Apply deposits to their agreed bill. Insurance is an inflow, not sold cargo.\\n\\nFor the default Loop net-share sponsor agreement:\\n\\n`SponsorBase = max(0, G − T − RQ)`\\n\\n`SponsorShare = contracted percentage × SponsorBase, rounded once as payment`\\n\\n`NetExpedition = G − T − RQ − SponsorShare`\\n\\nG is actual sale receipts, T the direct Haven taxes on them, and RQ the episode's actual mandatory rescue/quarantine expenses. Count RQ once whether paid earlier or now. This sponsor base is before personal wages and discretionary purchases. A signed gross-share or priority-lien agreement instead uses its own declared terms.\\n\\nTrue profit includes actual episode inflows less every distinct actual episode cost once: shares, wages, consumed/replaced supplies, operating fuel, repair, interest and other agreed costs. Show capital purchases and borrowed principal separately. Loans and principal repayment are financing, and selling existing personal capital is not earned cargo. Cash allocated to next-expedition reserves is a further allocation, not automatically the same as profit.\\n\\nReport the charter's gross cargo target, net expedition target and true-profit/distributable-after-reserves target, stating whether the comparison is an appraisal or a realized sale. Appraised value does not pay wages. Keep insufficiently funded obligations owed; do not invent negative inventory, a compulsory new loan or unlimited sponsor funds. Allocations cannot exceed actual available money.\"} traits names count script"
